# Agents · H · part 1

880 entries, h to hao-patches. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **h** ([odysseyalive/claude-workforce](https://github.com/odysseyalive/claude-workforce/blob/HEAD/fixtures/scripts/stamp-restamp-amd/.claude/agents/h.md) · ★2)
- **h-agent**: Routing target for `/h-mode` and any request for h's style. Resume an existing `h-agent` for the conversation rather than spawning a sibling. Reads the `h-mode` skill's `SKILL.md` in full before any work, including its inline Principles index. Substituting `general-purpose` skips that read and drifts. ([hsaegusa1112/hstack](https://github.com/hsaegusa1112/hstack/blob/HEAD/agents/h-agent.md) · ★1)
- **h-assembler**: Stage-4 sub-agent of the .H underwrite. Assembles the final &lt;CN&gt;.H (Review) tab from the three upstream outputs — merges the Census scratch workbook in, then builds Blocks A–E (occupancy links, detail passthrough via XLOOKUP, SUMIFS roll-up, metrics, S/T/U totals) per the h-underwrite skill's fixed template, runs the build-side gates, and saves. Spawned by the h-underwrite orchestrator; not for direct use. Returns a compact result (detailLast, rollupFirst, Check readings, flags). ([RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting](https://github.com/RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h-assembler.md))
- **h-verifier**: QA sub-agent of the .H underwrite. A VERIFIER, not a rebuilder: reopens the saved .H workbook fresh, independently recomputes the two reconciliation tie-outs and re-checks the cross-tab joins, and diffs against the operator's own subtotals. Spawned by the h-underwrite orchestrator after assembly + save. Different model from the builders by design — catches interpretation errors, not just arithmetic. ([RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting](https://github.com/RTM-Source/Agentic-Underwriting/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h-verifier.md))
- **h1-research-worker**: Research HackerOne program — scope, bounties, disclosed reports ([overtimepog/greyhatcc](https://github.com/overtimepog/greyhatcc/blob/HEAD/agents/h1-research-worker.md))
- **h22-adjudicator**: Runs the blueprint §10 intake protocol on external research artifacts (dossiers, whitepapers, threads, release notes). Produces a one-page adjudication appendix under docs/intake/ — tier-labeled claims, P1–P7 verdicts, harvest into existing gaps. Never revises the blueprint itself. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-adjudicator.md) · ★12)
- **h22-docsurgeon**: G7 public-claim-surface surgeon. Fixes doc drift on README.md and docs/ only — auth instructions, About/framing language, badge/version reconciliation, loopback-security sentence, C3 Moneta-naming consistency (one-Moneta ruling). Never touches code, tests, or state files. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-docsurgeon.md) · ★12)
- **h22-forge**: Implementation agent for gated H22 blueprint work — G1 port waves, G5 grounding tools, G9 gate build, baseline/freeze code. Refuses any dispatch that lacks a GATEWARDEN ALLOW. Works in worktrees, one atomic commit per wave, never merges or pushes. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-forge.md) · ★12)
- **h22-gatewarden**: Read-only gate-state oracle for the H22 blueprint harness. Reads drop.json, flywheel_queue.json, posture.json and the arming markers, then emits an ALLOW/REFUSE verdict per work item. Never flips a gate; never writes anything. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-gatewarden.md) · ★12)
- **h22-scribe**: Phase-0 paper author for the H22 blueprint. Drafts the Leg-0 specs (port-wave manifest, pre-flight gate, grounding contract, benchmark design) and freezes baselines. Writes ONLY under docs/ and harness/notes/. Every cited path, count, or symbol is verified by its own Read/Grep first. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-scribe.md) · ★12)

- **h5-art-director**: Picks the visual identity for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill — STYLE preset (candyPop / softCozy / neonArcade / sunsetGel / cleanMinimal / darkGlow or custom), display + body font pairing, palette (dominant + accents), shape language, and background atmosphere. Returns a concrete STYLE block ready to paste into the game. Use when the prompt is vague about aesthetic, when the user wants advice on what look fits, or when varying the look from a previous build. Do not use for game-mechanic / level-design questions. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-art-director.md))
- **h5-asset-prep**: Sources, optimizes, packs, and embeds art and audio assets for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. Finds CC0 packs (Kenney, Quaternius, etc.), verifies license, downloads, optimizes images to &lt;=1024 longest side, packs sprites into atlases, runs embed_asset.py to produce base64 data URIs, and returns the URIs + frame names ready to paste into the game. Use when the user wants real (sourced or supplied) art instead of procedural, or when an Assets/ folder needs ingestion. Do not use for procedural-only builds. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-asset-prep.md))
- **h5-bug-fixer**: Diagnoses and patches bugs in an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. Reads the game source, maps the symptom to the common-bugs.md catalog (45 cataloged breakages — camera, mobile fit, state machine, audio, memory, z-order), applies the smallest surgical patch, and re-verifies via lint. Use when an H5 game is broken or the user reports a specific symptom (camera wrong, retry double-starts, pause click-through, model floating, etc.). Do not use to build a game from scratch. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-bug-fixer.md))
- **h5-level-designer**: Designs the LEVELS data array for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. Tunes the difficulty curve (speed, spawn rate, gap, target, obstacleChance) so level 1 is trivially easy, the ramp is fair (~25% per level), and a final win condition exists. Returns a paste-ready LEVELS array. Use when starting a new build or when difficulty is wrong (player walls on level 2, levels feel identical, no win condition). Do not use to write game-loop code. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-level-designer.md))
- **h5-orchestrator**: Builds a full H5 game end-to-end in ONE delegation so the main agent's context stays clean. Consumes a resolved brief or applies one-line fastlane defaults, picks the visual identity + LEVELS inline, scaffolds from the golden template, and runs lint until it exits 0. IMPORTANT — it runs as a subagent, so it CANNOT dispatch other agents, ask the user follow-up questions, or run the browser QA gate; it returns a built+linted game plus an explicit "QA still required" flag, and the MAIN agent then dispatches h5-qa-tester. Use after intake is resolved, especially for a one-line build. Do not use f… ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-orchestrator.md))
- **h5-qa-tester**: Playtests an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. Runs lint, executes the game's built-in auto-QA harness (?h5qa=1 → H5QA_REPORT JSON) in a browser, takes a short visual-judgment screenshot pass, and returns a structured JSON verdict. Use when verifying a freshly built or patched H5 game before claiming done. Do not use for non-game web apps. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-qa-tester.md))
- **h5-smoke**: Fast static smoke-check for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill — runs lint.sh (chassis + node --check JS parse + wiring completeness), checks file size, and greps for leftover template identity, in ONE quick pass with NO browser. Returns a tight JSON verdict. Use for quick iteration between edits to catch breakage cheaply; it does NOT replace the full h5-qa-tester browser playtest, which is still required before claiming done. Do not use as the final gate. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-smoke.md))

([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-context-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-creator-agent**: Write-scoped PRODUCER BASE for one target Board Page. The workflow controller normally dispatches haipipe-draft-context-agent, haipipe-draft-structure-agent, haipipe-draft-evidence-agent, or haipipe-draft-writing-agent; this base keeps create-page/revise-opening and stands in only when a current producer is missing. It emits an auditable dispatch receipt, self-checks without approving, never touches board.md, never performs CHECK, and never settles a human decision. Trigger: write board page, revise board opening, Page producer fallback, CONTEXT OUTLINE EVIDENCE CONTENT. - **handoff-reviewer**: Compare the generated handoff with the source transcript and current workspace. Flag objectives or completion claims without evidence, edits whose final state was not checked, commands reported without results, stale task status, parse errors that affect the narrative, missing next actions, and sensitive values that escaped redaction. Return corrections and a clear ready/not-ready recommendation. ([batteryshark/skill-tap](https://github.com/batteryshark/skill-tap/blob/HEAD/skills/productivity/claude-session-handoff/agents/handoff-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **handoff-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks a CCP handoff package for completeness and safety before the user switches Claude profiles. Use after /ccp:handoff or /ccp:compact-handoff to validate the generated files. ([iamjairoescobar/claude-code](https://github.com/iamjairoescobar/claude-code/blob/HEAD/plugins/ccp/agents/handoff-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **handoff-reviewer**: Reviewer for the "main session talks, subagents work" setup. Given result note paths, re-checks the actual commits, diffs and verification against the briefs and reports only differences. Does not edit code. ([lumpen-mya/claude-handoff](https://github.com/lumpen-mya/claude-handoff/blob/HEAD/plugins/handoff/agents/handoff-reviewer.md))
- **handoff-reviewer**: Независимое ревью task_spec на гейте handoff-review — последняя проверка перед тем, как начнёт тратиться delivery-бюджет. Запускается на глубинах «только хребет» и «полная»; состав проверки зависит от глубины разреза. ([rombel00/game-processes](https://github.com/rombel00/game-processes/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-definition/agents/handoff-reviewer.md))
- **handoff-router**: Use this router when the task moves beyond safe completion inside this skill. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skills](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skills/blob/HEAD/skills_omni/rayden-use/agents/handoff-router.md) · ★173)

- **handoff-router**: Reads open handoff files and recommends whether Claude, Codex, or the user should own the next step. ([SpeciLiam/CodexSkills](https://github.com/SpeciLiam/CodexSkills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/handoff-router.md) · ★3)
- **handoff-runner**: Runner for the "main session talks, subagents work" setup (tier `runner`). Runs only the exact commands written in its brief — fixed commands that repeat — the same verbatim command set run several times or over several targets (more than 3 tool calls in total, or long output sent to a file), checkable by numbers/output: git status and logs, test or build runs reported as pass/fail counts, file existence and line counts, copying named files — and returns the numbers. No judgment, interpretation, summaries or code edits; stops and reports when anything is outside its conditions. ([lumpen-mya/claude-handoff](https://github.com/lumpen-mya/claude-handoff/blob/HEAD/plugins/handoff/agents/handoff-runner.md))
- **handoff-summarizer**: Compress current work state into a durable handoff. ([0langa/agent-handoff-cck](https://github.com/0langa/agent-handoff-cck/blob/HEAD/agents/handoff-summarizer.md))
- **handoff-summarizer**: Cheap utility agent that distills a builder's handoff report file into a ≤5-line summary for the orchestrator. The caller passes ONE file path (e.g. `.xenodot/handoffs/&lt;slug&gt;.md`); this agent reads it and returns a tight caveman digest — gate verdict, files, what works, what's open — so the orchestrator never loads the full report into its context. Use after a backgrounded builder (godot-dev, godot-refactor) finishes and wrote its handoff file. Read-only; never edits, never re-does work. ([arthur0n/xenodot-twin](https://github.com/arthur0n/xenodot-twin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/handoff-summarizer.md))

- **hanmao-lead**: หัวหน้าทีม/orchestrator ของโปรเจกต์หารเมา — ใช้เมื่องานที่สั่งกินหลายเลเยอร์ (UI + สูตรหาร + store/Supabase + เทส + เอกสาร) หรือเป็นฟีเจอร์ใหม่ทั้งก้อน. หน้าที่คือแตกงานเป็นชิ้นตามขอบเขตไฟล์ แล้วกระจายให้ agent เฉพาะทางทำขนานกัน ไม่ลงมือเขียนโค้ดฟีเจอร์เอง. Use PROACTIVELY for multi-layer feature work. ([dkongcomsci/hanmao](https://github.com/dkongcomsci/hanmao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hanmao-lead.md))
- **hanmao-reviewer**: ผู้ตรวจงานก่อนปิดงานของหารเมา — ใช้หลังแก้โค้ดเสร็จ เพื่อรัน typecheck/build/E2E และตรวจว่าโค้ดใหม่ทำตามกฎโปรเจกต์ (สูตรอยู่ใน split.ts, useStore เท่านั้น, baht(), colors, a11y, confirmRemove, กติกา *Ids ว่าง). อ่านและรันคำสั่งได้ แต่ไม่แก้ไฟล์ ([dkongcomsci/hanmao](https://github.com/dkongcomsci/hanmao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hanmao-reviewer.md))
- **hanmao-test**: ผู้เชี่ยวชาญการทดสอบของหารเมา (Playwright E2E + unit ของ logic) — ใช้เมื่อต้องเขียนเทสใหม่ให้ฟีเจอร์ที่เพิ่งทำ, แก้เทสที่พังเพราะ UI/สูตรเปลี่ยน, หรือไล่หาสาเหตุที่เทสแดง. ขอบเขต tests/** + playwright.config.ts เท่านั้น ([dkongcomsci/hanmao](https://github.com/dkongcomsci/hanmao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hanmao-test.md))
- **hanna-style-pr-reviewer**: Use this agent when you want a detailed PR review in Hanna's style after completing a logical chunk of code or before submitting a pull request. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: User has just finished implementing a new feature with tests and wants a review before creating a PR.\nuser: "I've just finished adding the billing feature with tests. Can you review it?"\nassistant: "Let me use the hanna-style-pr-reviewer agent to give you a thorough review of your changes."\n&lt;commentary&gt;The user is requesting a code review after completing work, which matches when this agent should be used.&lt;/comment… ([pcasaretto/nix-home](https://github.com/pcasaretto/nix-home/blob/HEAD/home-manager/modules/common/claude-code/agents/hanna-style-pr-reviewer.md))
- **hannah**: Hannah Sorensen, first-time pregnant Client on the portal (docs/personas/first-time-client.md). Ask her a question; she answers in her own voice from what the repo records about her, with sources. Continue the conversation with SendMessage. - **hannah-arendt-counsel**: Use this agent for political theory, the ethics of bureaucracy, the diagnosis of authoritarian drift, and the questions of what a public realm is, what thinking has to do with morality, and why a person who has stopped thinking is more dangerous than a person who is actively cruel. Modeled on Hannah Arendt — German-Jewish refugee, student of Heidegger and Jaspers, author of *The Origins of Totalitarianism*, *The Human Condition*, *Eichmann in Jerusalem*, and *On Revolution*. The political theorist who watched Germany teach itself, in 1933, that what it was doing was reasonable; who left; who… ([sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation](https://github.com/sethshoultes/great-minds-constellation/blob/HEAD/plugins/great-counsels/agents/hannah-arendt-counsel.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **hannibal**: Orchestrator for A(i)-Team missions ([theaiteam-dev/the-ai-team-plugin](https://github.com/theaiteam-dev/the-ai-team-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/hannibal.md) · ★1)
- **Hannibal (orchestrator)**: Use when you need to assess the current project state and decide what to do next. Coordinates work across the team: planner, designer, coder, reviewer, qa. Reads specs, memory, and codebase to determine the right next step. ([sinedied/gb-bug-defense](https://github.com/sinedied/gb-bug-defense/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/orchestrator.agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **hans**: Use this agent — Hans, an expert backend engineer — to design, build, and refactor server-side code: REST/RPC API endpoints, Next.js Route Handlers and Server Actions, Supabase/Postgres schema + migrations + RLS policies, data models, background jobs, and MCP servers (both authoring new MCP tools and integrating existing ones). Hans loves APIs and MCP, writes clean, efficient, well-typed code, and makes opinionated architectural calls he can defend. Reach for him when the work is on the backend/data/integration layer rather than UI. He WRITES code (not just review) and ships PR-ready changes.… ([doug-parks/agent-fleet](https://github.com/doug-parks/agent-fleet/blob/HEAD/archive/agents/hans.md))
